{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"wipes","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"wiping","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"wiped","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"wiped","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Melissa wiped her glasses with her shirt.","type":"example"},{"text":"I wiped the sweat from my brow with the back of my hand.","type":"example"},{"text":"Tom started to wipe his eyes.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To move an object over, maintaining contact, with the intention of removing some substance from the surface. (Compare rub.)"],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"You've wiped grease all over your shirt.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To smear (a substance) with this kind of motion."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To remove by rubbing; to rub off; to obliterate; usually followed by away, off, or out."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I had nothing to wipe my bum with.","type":"example"},{"text":"Even if you wipe very carefully, you still have to wash your hands afterward.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To clean (the anus, buttocks and/or genitals) after defecation or urination."],"tags":["ambitransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I accidentally wiped my hard drive.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To erase."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"glosses":["To make (a joint, as between pieces of lead pipe), by surrounding the junction with a mass of solder, applied in a plastic condition by means of a rag with which the solder is shaped by rubbing."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["business","construction","manufacturing","plumbing"]},{"examples":[{"text":"You should wipe that smirk off your face before the boss comes in.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To remove an expression from one's face."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"glosses":["To deperm (a ship)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To perform a transition in which one scene or slide is replaced with another over time along a horizontal axis, as if one scene or slide is a layer being slid off the other."],"topics":["media"]},{"glosses":["To hit or strike."],"tags":["UK","obsolete","slang"]},{"glosses":["To cheat; to defraud; to trick; usually followed by out."],"tags":["obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/waɪp/"}],"upstream_line":167940,"upstream_offset":786816455,"upstream_record_sha256":"6088adcd1929dcb445fe33c96d670120adf22bf0dca3affd5c959cb93fee9725","word":"wipe"}
